function hexToRgb(hex) {
  let h = hex.replace('#', '');
  if (h.length === 3) h = h.split('').map(c => c + c).join('');
  return {
    r: parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16),
    g: parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16),
    b: parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)
  };
}
function rgbToHsl(r, g, b) {
  r /= 255;
  g /= 255;
  b /= 255;
  const mx = Math.max(r, g, b),
    mn = Math.min(r, g, b),
    d = mx - mn;
  let h = 0;
  const l = (mx + mn) / 2;
  const s = d === 0 ? 0 : d / (1 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1));
  if (d !== 0) {
    if (mx === r) h = (g - b) / d % 6;else if (mx === g) h = (b - r) / d + 2;else h = (r - g) / d + 4;
    h = Math.round(h * 60);
    if (h < 0) h += 360;
  }
  return {
    h,
    s: Math.round(s * 100),
    l: Math.round(l * 100)
  };
}
function hsl(h, s, l) {
  return `hsl(${h}, ${s}%, ${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, l))}%)`;
}

// Build the full CSS override block for a given accent hex.
function buildAccentCSS(hex) {
  const {
    r,
    g,
    b
  } = hexToRgb(hex);
  const {
    h,
    s,
    l
  } = rgbToHsl(r, g, b);
  const sat = Math.max(s, 70);
  const dark = hsl(h, sat, Math.max(l - 16, 14));
  const light = hsl(h, sat, Math.min(l + 16, 88));
  const bg = `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, 0.12)`;
  const rot = Math.round(h - 258); // chat-pattern source art is purple (~258°)
  const iconRot = Math.round(h - 248); // designed PNG/SVG icons share a ~248° base hue
  return `
  .neura-admin {
    --aw-primary: ${hex} !important; --aw-primary-dark: ${dark} !important; --aw-primary-light: ${light} !important; --aw-primary-bg: ${bg} !important;
    --aw-accent: ${hex} !important; --aw-eu-primary: ${hex} !important; --aw-eu-primary-dark: ${dark} !important;
    --aw-bubble-sent: linear-gradient(135deg, ${light}, ${dark}) !important; --aw-bubble-sent-solid: ${hex} !important;
  }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-1 { background: ${hsl(h, sat, 62)} !important; }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-2 { background: ${hsl(h, Math.max(sat - 35, 30), 88)} !important; }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-3 { background: ${hex} !important; }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-4 { background: ${dark} !important; }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-5 { background: ${hsl(h, Math.max(sat - 30, 30), 86)} !important; }
  .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-6 { background: linear-gradient(135deg, ${hex}, ${light}) !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-1 { background: ${hsl(h, sat, 58)} !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-2 { background: ${hsl(h, sat, 52)} !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-3 { background: ${dark} !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-4 { background: ${hsl(h, sat, 60)} !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-5 { background: ${hex} !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .aw-glass-bg.admin-bg .blob-6 { background: linear-gradient(135deg, ${dark}, ${hex}) !important; }
  .neura-admin .aw-chat-pattern::before { filter: hue-rotate(${rot}deg) saturate(1.25) !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"].neura-admin .aw-chat-pattern::before,
  .neura-admin[data-theme="dark"] .aw-chat-pattern::before { filter: invert(1) hue-rotate(${rot + 180}deg) brightness(1.1) saturate(1.25) !important; }
  .neura-admin .nav-icon-img { filter: hue-rotate(${iconRot}deg) saturate(1.2) !important; }
  [data-theme="dark"] .neura-admin .nav-icon-img { filter: hue-rotate(${iconRot}deg) saturate(1.2) brightness(1.12) !important; }
  .neura-admin .letter-avatar { filter: hue-rotate(${iconRot}deg) saturate(1.12) !important; }
  `;
}

// Renders an agent's saved photo avatar if set, else the colored initial circle
function AgentCircle({
  agent,
  size,
  radius,
  fontSize,
  className = '',
  style = {}
}) {
  if (agent.avatar) {
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `flex-shrink-0 overflow-hidden ${className}`,
      style: {
        width: size,
        height: size,
        borderRadius: radius,
        ...style
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("img", {
      src: agent.avatar,
      alt: "",
      className: "w-full h-full",
      style: {
        objectFit: 'cover',
        objectPosition: 'center 18%'
      },
      draggable: false
    }));
  }
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: `flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 ${agent.bg} ${className}`,
    style: {
      width: size,
      height: size,
      borderRadius: radius,
      fontWeight: 700,
      fontSize: fontSize ?? Math.round(size * 0.42),
      ...style
    }
  }, agent.init);
}

function NavIcon({
  id,
  fa,
  size,
  active
}) {
  const src = NAV_ICON_SRC[id];
  if (src) return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("img", {
    src: src,
    alt: "",
    className: "nav-icon-img",
    style: {
      width: size + 4,
      height: size + 4,
      objectFit: 'contain',
      filter: active ? 'brightness(0) invert(1)' : undefined
    }
  });
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: fa,
    style: {
      fontSize: size,
      color: active ? '#fff' : undefined
    }
  });
}

// Custom RTL-friendly Recharts tooltip
function ChartTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
  label
}) {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2 border border-[var(--aw-border)]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-modal)',
      direction: 'rtl',
      boxShadow: 'var(--aw-shadow-sm)'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] mb-1"
  }, label), payload.map((p, i) => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    key: i,
    className: "text-[12px] flex items-center gap-1.5",
    style: {
      color: p.color,
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "w-2 h-2 rounded-full",
    style: {
      background: p.color
    }
  }), p.name, ": ", toFa(p.value))));
}

function HeaderBtn({
  icon,
  badge,
  onClick
}) {
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-[38px] h-[38px] rounded-[10px] bg-transparent border-none text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)] text-base cursor-pointer relative flex items-center justify-center transition-all hover:text-[var(--aw-primary)]",
    onClick: onClick
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: icon
  }), badge != null && badge > 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "absolute top-1 right-1 w-[18px] h-[18px] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-danger)',
      fontWeight: 700
    }
  }, toFa(badge)));
}
export function RoleSwitcher() {
  const {
    role,
    setRole,
    setBriefingSeen,
    theme
  } = useApp();
  const isGlass = theme === 'glass' || theme === 'dark';
  if (isGlass) {
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex gap-[3px] p-[3px] rounded-[9999px] border",
      style: {
        background: 'var(--aw-eu-nav-bg)',
        borderColor: 'var(--aw-eu-nav-border)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
        boxShadow: '0 4px 4px rgba(46,134,255,0.2)'
      }
    }, ['user', 'admin'].map(r => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      key: r,
      className: "flex-1 border-none rounded-[100px] cursor-pointer transition-all",
      style: role === r ? {
        background: 'var(--aw-eu-primary, #7B62FC)',
        color: '#fff',
        fontWeight: 700,
        padding: '7px 12px',
        fontSize: 12,
        boxShadow: 'inset 2px 2px 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.45), 2px 4px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'
      } : {
        background: 'transparent',
        color: 'var(--aw-text-secondary)',
        fontWeight: 500,
        padding: '7px 12px',
        fontSize: 12
      },
      onClick: () => {
        setRole(r);
        if (r === 'admin') setBriefingSeen(true);
      }
    }, r === 'admin' ? 'پنل مدیریت' : 'پنل شخصی')));
  }
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex gap-1 p-1 mx-4 mb-1.5 rounded-[10px]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    }
  }, ['admin', 'user'].map(r => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    key: r,
    className: `flex-1 py-1.5 px-2 border-none rounded-lg text-[12px] cursor-pointer transition-all ${role === r ? 'text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-transparent text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]'}`,
    style: role === r ? {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 600,
      boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(46,134,255,0.3)'
    } : {
      fontWeight: 600
    },
    onClick: () => {
      setRole(r);
      if (r === 'admin') setBriefingSeen(true);
    }
  }, r === 'admin' ? 'پنل مدیریت' : 'پنل شخصی')));
}

// ========================
// AGENT SELECTOR — unified dropdown: دستیار شخصی + management teams
// (replaces the panel toggle; switches role + active team)
// ========================
export function AgentSelector() {
  const {
    role,
    setRole,
    agentTeam,
    setAgentTeam,
    setAdminScreen,
    setBriefingSeen
  } = useApp();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [menuPos, setMenuPos] = useState(null);
  const trigRef = useRef(null);
  const order = ['secretary', 'marketing', 'finance', 'procurement', 'sales'];
  const ASSISTANT_ICON = 'src/icons/svg2/assistant.svg';
  const options = [{
    key: 'assistant',
    name: 'دستیار شخصی',
    desc: 'دستیار هوشمند شخصی شما',
    icon: ASSISTANT_ICON
  }, ...order.map(k => ({
    key: k,
    name: TEAM_CONFIGS[k].name,
    desc: TEAM_CONFIGS[k].desc,
    icon: TEAM_ICON_SRC[k] || ASSISTANT_ICON
  }))];
  const currentKey = role === 'user' ? 'assistant' : agentTeam || 'assistant';
  const current = options.find(o => o.key === currentKey) || options[0];
  // Blue (business) side ⇒ tint the portalled icons blue to match the .neura-admin scope they escape.
  const blueTheme = role === 'admin';
  const toggle = () => {
    if (open) {
      setOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    const r = trigRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r) setMenuPos({
      top: r.bottom + 8,
      right: Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - r.right)
    });
    setOpen(true);
  };
  const pick = key => {
    setOpen(false);
    if (key === 'assistant') {
      // Personal assistant = the full consumer (EU) experience with its own
      // home screen, menus and pages. Restore the original behaviour.
      setRole('user');
      return;
    }
    setBriefingSeen(true);
    setAgentTeam(key);
    const cfg = TEAM_CONFIGS[key];
    if (cfg?.navItems?.[0]) setAdminScreen(cfg.navItems[0].id);
    setRole('admin');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "relative",
    style: {
      zIndex: 40
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    ref: trigRef,
    onClick: toggle,
    className: "flex items-center gap-1.5 pl-2.5 pr-2 py-1.5 rounded-full border cursor-pointer transition-all",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-eu-nav-bg, rgba(255,255,255,0.2))',
      borderColor: 'var(--aw-eu-nav-border, rgba(255,255,255,0.5))',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
      boxShadow: '0 4px 12px var(--aw-eu-primary-shadow, rgba(0,0,0,0.16))'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("img", {
    src: current.icon,
    alt: "",
    className: "nav-icon-img",
    style: {
      width: 22,
      height: 22,
      objectFit: 'contain'
    }
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "text-[12px] whitespace-nowrap",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 700,
      color: 'var(--aw-text-primary)'
    }
  }, current.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-chevron-down text-[9px]",
    style: {
      color: 'var(--aw-text-secondary)',
      transform: open ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'none',
      transition: 'transform .2s'
    }
  })), open && menuPos && /*#__PURE__*/createPortal(/*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: blueTheme ? 'neura-admin' : undefined,
    style: {
      position: 'fixed',
      inset: 0,
      zIndex: 9998
    },
    onClick: () => setOpen(false)
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(motion.div, {
    className: "w-[224px] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden p-1.5",
    onClick: e => e.stopPropagation(),
    style: {
      position: 'fixed',
      top: menuPos.top,
      right: menuPos.right,
      background: 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--aw-bg-modal) 96%, transparent)',
      border: '1px solid var(--aw-border)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(1.4)',
      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(1.4)',
      boxShadow: '0 16px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.32)',
      zIndex: 9999
    },
    initial: {
      opacity: 0,
      y: -8,
      scale: 0.96
    },
    animate: {
      opacity: 1,
      y: 0,
      scale: 1
    },
    transition: {
      duration: 0.18
    }
  }, options.map(o => {
    const isActive = o.key === currentKey;
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      key: o.key,
      onClick: () => pick(o.key),

// ========================
// TEAM ICON BAR (small icons for quick team switching)
// ========================
function TeamIconBar() {
  const {
    agentTeam,
    setAgentTeam,
    setAdminScreen,
    setRole,
    setBriefingSeen
  } = useApp();
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const teamOrder = ['secretary', 'marketing', 'finance', 'procurement', 'sales'];
  const teams = teamOrder.map(k => TEAM_CONFIGS[k]);
  const activeTeam = agentTeam ? TEAM_CONFIGS[agentTeam] : null;

  // Collapsed: show only the active agent + an expand button (saves vertical space)
  if (collapsed && activeTeam) {
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mx-4 mb-2 py-1.5"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full",
      style: {
        background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)',
        border: '1px solid var(--aw-border)'
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[12px]",
      style: {
        background: activeTeam.gradient
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: activeTeam.icon
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      className: "text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)]",
      style: {
        fontWeight: 700
      }
    }, activeTeam.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      className: "w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-transparent border-none cursor-pointer text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-[11px]",
      title: "\u0646\u0645\u0627\u06CC\u0634 \u0647\u0645\u0647 \u0639\u0627\u0645\u0644\u200C\u0647\u0627",
      onClick: () => setCollapsed(false)
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-chevron-down"
    }))));
  }
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mx-4 mb-2 py-2 relative"
  }, activeTeam && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "absolute left-1 top-1 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-transparent border-none cursor-pointer text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-[11px]",
    title: "\u062C\u0645\u0639 \u06A9\u0631\u062F\u0646 \u0646\u0648\u0627\u0631 \u0639\u0627\u0645\u0644\u200C\u0647\u0627",
    onClick: () => setCollapsed(true)
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-chevron-up"
  })), teams.map(team => {
    const active = agentTeam === team.id;
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      key: team.id,
      title: team.name,
      className: "relative flex flex-col items-center gap-1 bg-transparent border-none cursor-pointer p-0 group",
      style: {
        width: 56
      },
      onClick: () => {
        setAgentTeam(team.id);
        setAdminScreen(team.navItems[0]?.id || 'chatsScreen');
        setRole('admin');
        setBriefingSeen(true);
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `w-[42px] h-[42px] rounded-[12px] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[17px] transition-all ${active ? '' : 'opacity-50 hover:opacity-90'}`,
      style: {
        background: team.gradient,
        boxShadow: active ? '0 0 0 2px var(--aw-bg-app), 0 0 0 3.5px var(--aw-primary), 0 3px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' : 'none'
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: team.icon
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      className: `text-[10px] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${active ? 'text-[var(--aw-text-primary)]' : 'text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]'}`,
      style: {
        fontWeight: active ? 700 : 500
      }
    }, team.name));
  }));
}

// ========================
// NEW GROUP CHAT CONTENT (global, not tied to category)
// ========================
function NewGroupChatContent() {
  const {
    agents,
    personnel,
    customers,
    company,
    addGroupChat,
    closeModal,
    showToast,
    agentTeam,
    openChat
  } = useApp();
  const [chatName, setChatName] = useState('');
  const [selectOpen, setSelectOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchQ, setSearchQ] = useState('');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const companyAgents = agents.filter(a => !a.company || a.company === company).filter(a => !a.locked);
  const allItems = [...companyAgents.map(a => ({
    id: a.id,
    type: 'agent',
    name: a.name,
    sub: a.role,
    init: a.init,
    bg: a.bg
  })), ...personnel.map(p => ({
    id: p.id,
    type: 'personnel',
    name: p.name,
    sub: p.role,
    init: p.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-blue-500'
  })), ...customers.map(c => ({
    id: c.id,
    type: 'customer',
    name: c.name,
    sub: c.contact,
    init: c.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-emerald-500'
  }))];
  const filtered = allItems.filter(i => !searchQ || i.name.includes(searchQ) || i.sub.includes(searchQ));
  const isSelected = (id, type) => selected.some(s => s.id === id && s.type === type);
  const toggle = it => {
    setSelected(prev => isSelected(it.id, it.type) ? prev.filter(s => !(s.id === it.id && s.type === it.type)) : [...prev, {
      id: it.id,
      type: it.type,
      name: it.name,
      init: it.init,
      bg: it.bg
    }]);
  };
  const BG_OPTIONS = ['bg-violet-600', 'bg-pink-500', 'bg-blue-600', 'bg-emerald-600', 'bg-amber-600', 'bg-rose-600', 'bg-cyan-600'];
  const submit = () => {
    if (!chatName.trim()) {
      showToast('نام گروه را وارد کنید');
      return;
    }
    if (selected.length < 2) {
      showToast('حداقل ۲ عضو انتخاب کنید');
      return;
    }
    const id = 'gc_' + Date.now();
    addGroupChat({
      id,
      name: chatName.trim(),
      bg: BG_OPTIONS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BG_OPTIONS.length)],
      memberIds: selected.map(s => ({
        id: s.id,
        type: s.type
      })),
      team: agentTeam || null
    });
    showToast('چت گروهی ایجاد شد');
    closeModal();
    setTimeout(() => openChat(id, 'group'), 150);
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-col gap-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)] mb-1.5 block",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, "\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u06AF\u0631\u0648\u0647"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "w-full bg-[var(--aw-bg-input)] border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    placeholder: "\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B: \u062A\u06CC\u0645 \u067E\u0631\u0648\u0698\u0647...",
    value: chatName,
    onChange: e => setChatName(e.target.value)
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)] mb-1.5 block",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, "\u0645\u062E\u0627\u0637\u0628\u06CC\u0646"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "w-full bg-[var(--aw-bg-input)] border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between gap-2",
    onClick: () => setSelectOpen(o => !o)
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-wrap gap-1 flex-1 min-h-[20px]"
  }, selected.length === 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }, "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0645\u062E\u0627\u0637\u0628\u06CC\u0646..."), selected.map(s => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    key: s.type + s.id,
    className: "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] text-white",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)'
    }
  }, s.name, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "border-none bg-transparent text-white cursor-pointer text-[10px] p-0 leading-none",
    onClick: e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      toggle({
        id: s.id,
        type: s.type,
        name: s.name,
        init: s.init,
        bg: s.bg
      });
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-times"
  }))))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: `fa-solid fa-chevron-${selectOpen ? 'up' : 'down'} text-[10px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]`
  })), selectOpen && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "mt-1.5 border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] overflow-hidden",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "w-full bg-transparent border-b border-[var(--aw-border)] px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    placeholder: "\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648...",
    value: searchQ,
    onChange: e => setSearchQ(e.target.value),
    autoFocus: true
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "max-h-[240px] overflow-y-auto aw-scroll"
  }, filtered.length === 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-center py-4 text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-[12px]"
  }, "\u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F"), filtered.map(it => {
    const sel = isSelected(it.id, it.type);
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      key: it.type + it.id,
      className: "flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--aw-bg-card-hover)]",
      onClick: () => toggle(it)
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `w-7 h-7 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[11px] flex-shrink-0 ${it.bg}`,
      style: {
        fontWeight: 700
      }
    }, it.init), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[12px]",
      style: {
        fontWeight: 600
      }
    }, it.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[10px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] truncate"
    }, it.sub)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `w-4 h-4 rounded flex items-center justify-center ${sel ? 'text-white' : ''}`,

      }
    }, sel && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-check text-[8px]"
    })));
  })))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-full py-3 rounded-[10px] border-none text-white cursor-pointer text-[13px]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 700
    },
    onClick: submit
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-check ml-1.5"
  }), "\u0627\u06CC\u062C\u0627\u062F \u0686\u062A \u06AF\u0631\u0648\u0647\u06CC"));
}

// ========================
// RENAME CATEGORY
// ========================
function RenameCategoryContent({
  group
}) {
  const {
    updateChatGroup,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const [name, setName] = useState(group.name);
  const submit = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      showToast('نام معتبر وارد کنید');
      return;
    }
    updateChatGroup(group.id, {
      name: name.trim()
    });
    showToast('نام دسته‌بندی تغییر یافت');
    closeModal();
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-col gap-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)]",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, "\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "w-full bg-[var(--aw-bg-input)] border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none",
    value: name,
    onChange: e => setName(e.target.value),
    autoFocus: true
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-full py-3 rounded-[10px] border-none text-white cursor-pointer text-[13px]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 700
    },
    onClick: submit
  }, "\u0630\u062E\u06CC\u0631\u0647"));
}

// ========================
// ADD CHAT TO CATEGORY
// ========================
function AddChatToCategoryContent({
  group
}) {
  const {
    agents,
    personnel,
    customers,
    company,
    updateChatGroup,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const [searchQ, setSearchQ] = useState('');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const isMember = (id, type) => group.members.some(m => m.id === id && m.type === type);
  const isSelected = (id, type) => selected.some(s => s.id === id && s.type === type);
  const companyAgents = agents.filter(a => !a.company || a.company === company).filter(a => !a.locked);
  const allItems = [...companyAgents.map(a => ({
    id: a.id,
    type: 'agent',
    name: a.name,
    sub: a.role,
    init: a.init,
    bg: a.bg
  })), ...personnel.map(p => ({
    id: p.id,
    type: 'personnel',
    name: p.name,
    sub: p.role,
    init: p.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-blue-500'
  })), ...customers.map(c => ({
    id: c.id,
    type: 'customer',
    name: c.name,
    sub: c.contact,
    init: c.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-emerald-500'
  }))].filter(i => !isMember(i.id, i.type));
  const filtered = allItems.filter(i => !searchQ || i.name.includes(searchQ) || i.sub.includes(searchQ));
  const toggle = (id, type) => {
    setSelected(prev => prev.some(s => s.id === id && s.type === type) ? prev.filter(s => !(s.id === id && s.type === type)) : [...prev, {
      id,
      type
    }]);
  };
  const submit = () => {
    if (selected.length === 0) {
      showToast('چتی انتخاب نشده');
      return;
    }
    updateChatGroup(group.id, {
      members: [...group.members, ...selected]
    });
    showToast(toFa(selected.length) + ' چت اضافه شد');
    closeModal();
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-col gap-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "w-full bg-[var(--aw-bg-input)] border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    placeholder: "\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648...",
    value: searchQ,
    onChange: e => setSearchQ(e.target.value)
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto aw-scroll flex flex-col gap-1"
  }, filtered.length === 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-center py-6 text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-[12px]"
  }, "\u0686\u062A\u06CC \u0628\u0631\u0627\u06CC \u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 \u0646\u06CC\u0633\u062A"), filtered.map(it => {
    const sel = isSelected(it.id, it.type);
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      key: it.type + '_' + it.id,
      className: `flex items-center gap-2.5 p-2.5 rounded-[10px] cursor-pointer transition-all border ${sel ? 'border-[var(--aw-primary)]' : 'border-transparent'}`,
      style: {
        background: sel ? 'rgba(126,95,170,0.12)' : 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
      },
      onClick: () => toggle(it.id, it.type)
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `w-9 h-9 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 ${it.bg}`,
      style: {
        fontWeight: 700
      }
    }, it.init), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[13px]",
      style: {
        fontWeight: 600
      }
    }, it.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] truncate"
    }, it.sub)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: `w-5 h-5 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 ${sel ? 'text-white' : ''}`,
      style: {
        background: sel ? 'var(--aw-primary)' : 'var(--aw-bg-card)',
        border: sel ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--aw-border)'
      }
    }, sel && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-check text-[10px]"
    })));
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-full py-3 rounded-[10px] border-none text-white cursor-pointer text-[13px]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 700
    },
    onClick: submit
  }, "\u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 ", selected.length > 0 && '(' + toFa(selected.length) + ')'));
}

// ========================
// NEW GROUP CONTENT (for + button modal)
// ========================
function NewGroupContent() {
  const {
    agents,
    personnel,
    customers,
    company,
    addChatGroup,
    setChatTab,
    closeModal,
    showToast,
    agentTeam
  } = useApp();
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState([]);
  const [searchQ, setSearchQ] = useState('');
  const companyAgents = agents.filter(a => !a.company || a.company === company).filter(a => !a.locked);
  const allItems = [...companyAgents.map(a => ({
    id: a.id,
    type: 'agent',
    name: a.name,
    sub: a.role,
    init: a.init,
    bg: a.bg
  })), ...personnel.map(p => ({
    id: p.id,
    type: 'personnel',
    name: p.name,
    sub: p.role,
    init: p.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-blue-500'
  })), ...customers.map(c => ({
    id: c.id,
    type: 'customer',
    name: c.name,
    sub: c.contact,
    init: c.name[0],
    bg: 'bg-emerald-500'
  }))];
  const filtered = allItems.filter(i => !searchQ || i.name.includes(searchQ) || i.sub.includes(searchQ));
  const toggleSelect = (id, type) => {
    setSelected(prev => {
      const exists = prev.find(s => s.id === id && s.type === type);
      if (exists) return prev.filter(s => !(s.id === id && s.type === type));
      return [...prev, {
        id,
        type
      }];
    });
  };
  const isSelected = (id, type) => selected.some(s => s.id === id && s.type === type);
  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!groupName.trim()) {
      showToast('لطفاً نام دسته‌بندی را وارد کنید');
      return;
    }
    if (selected.length === 0) {
      showToast('لطفاً حداقل یک چت انتخاب کنید');
      return;
    }
    const group = {
      id: 'grp_' + Date.now(),
      name: groupName.trim(),
      members: selected,
      team: agentTeam || null
    };
    addChatGroup(group);
    setChatTab(group.id);
    closeModal();
    showToast('دسته‌بندی «' + groupName.trim() + '» ایجاد شد');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-col gap-4 max-h-[70vh]"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "block text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] mb-1.5"
  }, "\u0646\u0627\u0645 \u062F\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC \u062C\u062F\u06CC\u062F"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "w-full bg-transparent border border-[var(--aw-border)] rounded-[10px] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
    },
    placeholder: "\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B: \u0645\u0634\u062A\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0646 VIP",
    value: groupName,
    onChange: e => setGroupName(e.target.value),
    autoFocus: true
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: "block text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] mb-1.5"
  }, "\u0627\u0641\u0632\u0648\u062F\u0646 \u0686\u062A\u200C\u0647\u0627 \u0628\u0647 \u0627\u06CC\u0646 \u06AF\u0631\u0648\u0647", selected.length > 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "mr-1 text-[var(--aw-primary)]"
  }, "(", toFa(selected.length), " \u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0634\u062F\u0647)")), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] px-3 border border-[var(--aw-border)] mb-2",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-search text-sm text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "flex-1 bg-transparent border-none py-2 text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    placeholder: "\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648 \u062F\u0631 \u0645\u062E\u0627\u0637\u0628\u06CC\u0646...",
    value: searchQ,
    onChange: e => setSearchQ(e.target.value)
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "overflow-y-auto max-h-[40vh] aw-scroll rounded-[10px] border border-[var(--aw-border)]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
    }
  }, filtered.length === 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-center py-6 text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-[12px]"
  }, "\u0645\u0648\u0631\u062F\u06CC \u06CC\u0627\u0641\u062A \u0646\u0634\u062F"), filtered.map(item => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    key: item.type + '_' + item.id,
    className: "w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 border-none cursor-pointer transition-all bg-transparent text-right",
    style: {
      borderBottom: '1px solid var(--aw-border)',
      background: isSelected(item.id, item.type) ? 'rgba(126,95,170,0.15)' : 'transparent'
    },
    onClick: () => toggleSelect(item.id, item.type)
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "relative"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: `w-9 h-9 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 ${item.bg}`,
    style: {
      fontWeight: 700,
      fontSize: 13
    }
  }, item.init), isSelected(item.id, item.type) && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[8px] text-white",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-check"
  }))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] truncate"
  }, item.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] truncate"
  }, item.sub)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full",
    style: {
      background: item.type === 'agent' ? 'rgba(126,95,170,0.2)' : item.type === 'personnel' ? 'rgba(59,130,246,0.2)' : 'rgba(0,230,118,0.2)',
      color: item.type === 'agent' ? '#9F8DC6' : item.type === 'personnel' ? '#3B82F6' : '#00E676'
    }
  }, item.type === 'agent' ? 'عامل' : item.type === 'personnel' ? 'پرسنل' : 'مشتری'))))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-full py-3 rounded-[10px] border-none text-white cursor-pointer text-[13px] transition-all",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 600,
      opacity: groupName.trim() && selected.length > 0 ? 1 : 0.5
    },
    onClick: handleCreate
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-folder-plus ml-2"
  }), "\u0627\u06CC\u062C\u0627\u062F \u06AF\u0631\u0648\u0647 (", toFa(selected.length), " \u0639\u0636\u0648)"));
}

const SearchHighlightContext = /*#__PURE__*/createContext('');
function Hi({
  text
}) {
  const q = useContext(SearchHighlightContext).trim();
  if (!q || !text) return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(React.Fragment, null, text);
  const idx = text.indexOf(q);
  if (idx < 0) return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(React.Fragment, null, text);
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(React.Fragment, null, text.slice(0, idx), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("mark", {
    style: {
      background: 'rgba(255,210,0,0.45)',
      color: 'inherit',
      borderRadius: 3,
      padding: '0 1px'
    }
  }, text.slice(idx, idx + q.length)), text.slice(idx + q.length));
}

function PersonnelPeerChatViewer({
  chat,
  personnel
}) {
  const memberNames = chat.members.map(id => personnel.find(p => p.id === id)?.name || id);
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex flex-col",
    style: {
      maxHeight: '70vh'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "px-1 pb-3 border-b border-[var(--aw-border)] mb-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[13px] mb-1",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, chat.isGroup ? chat.title : memberNames.join(' و ')), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] flex items-center gap-1.5"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-eye"
  }), "\u0641\u0642\u0637 \u0645\u0634\u0627\u0647\u062F\u0647 \u2014 ", chat.isGroup ? `${toFa(chat.members.length)} نفر` : 'گفتگوی دونفره')), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1 overflow-y-auto aw-scroll space-y-2 pb-2"
  }, chat.transcript.map((m, i) => {
    const sender = personnel.find(p => p.id === m.from);
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      key: i,
      className: "flex gap-2 items-start"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[10px] flex-shrink-0 bg-[var(--aw-primary)]",
      style: {
        fontWeight: 700
      }
    }, sender?.name?.[0] || '؟'), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      style: {
        fontWeight: 600
      }
    }, sender?.name || m.from), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", null, "\xB7"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", null, m.time)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "text-[12px] rounded-[10px] p-2.5 inline-block border border-[var(--aw-border)]",
      style: {
        background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
      }
    }, m.text)));
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--aw-border)] text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-lock"
  }), "\u062F\u0633\u062A\u0631\u0633\u06CC \u0627\u062F\u0645\u06CC\u0646 \u0641\u0642\u0637 \u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0646\u06CC \u0627\u0633\u062A \u2014 \u0627\u0645\u06A9\u0627\u0646 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0627\u0644 \u067E\u06CC\u0627\u0645 \u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F"));
}
function ChatsScreen() {
  const {
    chatTab,
    setChatTab,
    agents,
    personnel,
    customers,
    company,
    openChat,
    openModal,
    chatGroups,
    removeChatGroup,
    updateChatGroup,
    showToast,
    agentTeam,
    startCall,
    groupChats,
    removeGroupChat
  } = useApp();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [hiddenBaseTabs, setHiddenBaseTabs] = useState({});
  const listScrollRef = useRef(null);
  const [spyTab, setSpyTab] = useState('customers');
  const onListScroll = () => {
    const sc = listScrollRef.current;
    if (!sc || chatTab !== 'all') return;
    const markers = sc.querySelectorAll('[data-cat]');
    const topY = sc.getBoundingClientRect().top;
    let current = '';
    markers.forEach(m => {
      const r = m.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.top - topY <= 72) current = m.getAttribute('data-cat') || current;
    });
    if (current && current !== spyTab) setSpyTab(current);
  };
  const teamScope = agentTeam || '_none';
  const hiddenForTeam = hiddenBaseTabs[teamScope] || [];
  const hideBaseTab = id => {
    setHiddenBaseTabs(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamScope]: [...(prev[teamScope] || []), id]
    }));
    if (chatTab === id) setChatTab('all');
    showToast('دسته‌بندی حذف شد');
  };
  const allBaseTabs = [{
    id: 'customers',
    label: 'تعاملات من'
  }, {
    id: 'agents',
    label: 'عامل هوشمند'
  }, {
    id: 'personnel',
    label: 'تعاملات پرسنل'
  }, ...(agentTeam === 'secretary' ? [{
    id: 'meetings',
    label: 'جلسات'
  }] : [])];
  const baseTabs = allBaseTabs.filter(t => !hiddenForTeam.includes(t.id));
  const allTabs = baseTabs;
  const filteredAgents = agents.filter(a => !a.company || a.company === company).filter(a => !search || a.name.includes(search) || a.role.includes(search));
  const filteredPersonnel = personnel.filter(p => !search || p.name.includes(search) || p.role.includes(search));
  const filteredCustomers = customers.filter(c => !search || c.name.includes(search) || c.contact.includes(search));
  const teamKey = agentTeam || null;
  const teamSamples = teamKey ? TEAM_SAMPLE_CHATS[teamKey] : null;
  const teamConfig = teamKey ? TEAM_CONFIGS[teamKey] : null;
  const filteredTeamSamples = teamSamples?.filter(s => !search || s.name.includes(search) || s.lastMsg.includes(search) || s.tag.includes(search)) || [];

  // Find current group if active tab is a custom group
  const activeGroup = chatGroups.find(g => g.id === chatTab);

  // Get members for a group
  const getGroupMembers = group => {
    return group.members.map(m => {
      if (m.type === 'agent') {
        const a = agents.find(x => x.id === m.id);
        return a ? {
          ...m,
          name: a.name,
          sub: a.role,
          init: a.init,
          bg: a.bg
        } : null;
      }
      if (m.type === 'personnel') {
        const p = personnel.find(x => x.id === m.id);
        return p ? {
          ...m,
          name: p.name,
          sub: p.role,
          init: p.name[0],
          bg: 'bg-blue-500'
        } : null;
      }
      if (m.type === 'customer') {
        const c = customers.find(x => x.id === m.id);
        return c ? {

function fmtRial(n) {
  return toFa(Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US'));
}
function nowDateFa() {
  // Simple current Persian-style date placeholder (Jalali approximation for demo).
  return '۱۴۰۴/۱۲/۰۵';
}
function invoiceTotals(inv) {
  const subtotal = inv.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.qty || 0) * (l.unitPrice || 0), 0);
  const taxable = Math.max(0, subtotal - (inv.discount || 0));
  const tax = Math.round(taxable * (inv.taxPct || 0) / 100);
  const total = taxable + tax;
  return {
    subtotal,
    tax,
    total
  };
}

  }
};

function InvoiceDetail({
  invId
}) {
  const {
    invoices,
    showToast,
    closeModal
  } = useApp();
  const inv = invoices.find(i => i.id === invId);
  if (!inv) return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

function NewInvoiceForm() {
  const {
    addInvoice,
    showToast,
    closeModal,
    invoices
  } = useApp();
  const [type, setType] = useState('sales');
  const [customer, setCustomer] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [issueDate, setIssueDate] = useState('');
  const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState('');
  const [taxPct, setTaxPct] = useState('9');
  const [discount, setDiscount] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('pending');
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [lines, setLines] = useState([{
    desc: '',
    qty: '1',
    unitPrice: ''
  }]);
  const faToEn = s => s.replace(/[۰-۹]/g, d => String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d))).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '');
  const num = s => parseFloat(faToEn(s)) || 0;
  const subtotal = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + num(l.qty) * num(l.unitPrice), 0);
  const disc = num(discount);
  const tax = Math.round(Math.max(0, subtotal - disc) * num(taxPct) / 100);
  const total = Math.max(0, subtotal - disc) + tax;
  const updateLine = (i, key, val) => setLines(prev => prev.map((l, idx) => idx === i ? {
    ...l,
    [key]: val
  } : l));
  const addLine = () => setLines(prev => [...prev, {
    desc: '',
    qty: '1',
    unitPrice: ''
  }]);
  const removeLine = i => setLines(prev => prev.length > 1 ? prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i) : prev);
  const nextNumber = () => {
    const prefix = type === 'sales' ? 'INV-' : 'PUR-';
    const nums = invoices.filter(i => i.number.startsWith(prefix)).map(i => parseInt(i.number.replace(prefix, '')) || 0);
    const max = nums.length ? Math.max(...nums) : type === 'sales' ? 1405 : 312;
    return prefix + (max + 1);
  };
  const valid = customer.trim() && lines.some(l => l.desc.trim() && num(l.unitPrice) > 0);
  const submit = () => {
    if (!valid) {
      showToast('نام مشتری و حداقل یک قلم با قیمت لازم است', 'error');
      return;
    }
    const inv = {
      id: 'inv_' + Date.now(),
      number: nextNumber(),
      type,
      customer: customer.trim(),
      customerPhone: phone.trim() || undefined,
      issueDate: issueDate.trim() || nowDateFa(),
      dueDate: dueDate.trim() || nowDateFa(),
      lines: lines.filter(l => l.desc.trim()).map(l => ({
        desc: l.desc.trim(),
        qty: num(l.qty) || 1,
        unitPrice: num(l.unitPrice)
      })),
      taxPct: num(taxPct),
      discount: disc,
      status,
      note: note.trim() || undefined
    };
    addInvoice(inv);
    showToast(`فاکتور ${toFa(inv.number)} ثبت شد ✅`, 'success');
    closeModal();
  };
  const lbl = 'text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)] mb-1 block';
  const inputCls = 'w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-[10px] text-[13px] border border-[var(--aw-border)] bg-[var(--aw-bg-input)] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none';
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "p-4"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex gap-1 p-[3px] rounded-full border border-[var(--aw-border)] mb-3",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    }
  }, [{
    id: 'sales',
    label: 'فاکتور فروش'
  }, {
    id: 'purchase',
    label: 'فاکتور خرید'
  }].map(t => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    key: t.id,
    className: "flex-1 py-2 rounded-full border-none cursor-pointer text-[12px]",
    style: type === t.id ? {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary)',
      color: '#fff',
      fontWeight: 700
    } : {
      background: 'transparent',
      color: 'var(--aw-text-muted)',
      fontWeight: 600
    },
    onClick: () => setType(t.id)
  }, t.label))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "mb-2"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: lbl
  }, type === 'sales' ? 'نام مشتری' : 'نام تأمین‌کننده', " *"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: inputCls,
    value: customer,
    onChange: e => setCustomer(e.target.value),
    placeholder: "\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B \u0634\u0631\u06A9\u062A \u067E\u0627\u0631\u0633 \u0641\u0648\u0644\u0627\u062F"
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "mb-2"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("label", {
    className: lbl

    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {

      onClick: () => {
        if (unlocked) setAgentAccent(c);else openModal('خرید تم رنگی', /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(BuyItemModal, {
          kind: "theme",
          label: "\u062A\u0645 \u0631\u0646\u06AF\u06CC \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0635\u06CC",
          price: THEME_PRICE,
          swatch: c,
          onConfirm: () => {
            buyTheme(c, THEME_PRICE);
            setAgentAccent(c);
          }
        }));
      },
      className: "w-9 h-9 rounded-full cursor-pointer p-0 flex items-center justify-center relative",
      style: {
        background: c,
        border: agentAccent === c ? '2.5px solid var(--aw-text-primary)' : '2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35)',

      }
    }, agentAccent === c && unlocked && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-check text-[12px] text-white",
      style: {
        textShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)'
      }
    }), !unlocked && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-lock text-[10px] text-white",
      style: {
        textShadow: '0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'
      }
    }));
  }))));
}

  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');

  const [todoItems, setTodoItems] = useState(INITIAL_TODO_ITEMS);
  const [newTodo, setNewTodo] = useState('');
  const [selectedDay, setSelectedDay] = useState(null);
  const [plannerDay, setPlannerDay] = useState('today');
  const [plannerBlocks, setPlannerBlocks] = useState(PLANNER_BLOCKS_INIT);
  const filteredTasks = tasks.filter(t => filter === 'all' || t.status === filter).filter(t => !search || t.title.includes(search) || t.assignee.includes(search) || t.description.includes(search));
  const todoCount = tasks.filter(t => t.status === 'todo').length;
  const inProgressCount = tasks.filter(t => t.status === 'inProgress').length;
  const doneCount = tasks.filter(t => t.status === 'done').length;
  const filters = [{
    id: 'all',
    label: 'همه',
    count: tasks.length
  }, {

      id: 'td' + Date.now(),

      createdAt: 'الان'

function PlannerAddContent({

      id: 'pb' + Date.now(),
      time,
      title,
      category,
      duration: duration || '۳۰ دقیقه'
    });

    showToast('بلاک جدید اضافه شد');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {

    label: "\u0633\u0627\u0639\u062A \u0634\u0631\u0648\u0639"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormInput, {
    placeholder: "\u0645\u062B\u0644\u0627\u064B \u06F1\u06F0:\u06F0\u06F0",

function TaskCard({
  task
}) {
  const {
    openModal,
    setTasks,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const statusColor = TASK_STATUS_COLORS[task.status];
  const priorityColor = TASK_PRIORITY_COLORS[task.priority];
  const toggleDone = e => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const newStatus = task.status === 'done' ? 'todo' : 'done';
    setTasks(prev => prev.map(t => t.id === task.id ? {
      ...t,
      status: newStatus
    } : t));
    showToast(newStatus === 'done' ? 'وظیفه تکمیل شد' : 'وظیفه به حالت انتظار بازگشت');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: `rounded-[10px] p-3.5 mb-2 border border-[var(--aw-border)] cursor-pointer transition-all hover:border-[var(--aw-primary)] ${task.status === 'done' ? 'opacity-70' : ''}`,
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    },
    onClick: () => openModal(task.title, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(TaskDetailContent, {
      taskId: task.id
    }))
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-start gap-3"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 cursor-pointer transition-all",
    style: {
      borderColor: task.status === 'done' ? '#10b981' : 'var(--aw-border)',
      background: task.status === 'done' ? '#10b981' : 'transparent'
    },
    onClick: toggleDone
  }, task.status === 'done' && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-check text-[10px] text-white"
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex justify-between items-start mb-1.5"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {

function TaskDetailContent({
  taskId
}) {
  const {
    tasks,
    setTasks,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const t = tasks.find(x => x.id === taskId);
  if (!t) return null;
  const update = (field, val) => {
    setTasks(prev => prev.map(tk => tk.id === taskId ? {
      ...tk,
      [field]: val
    } : tk));
  };
  const handleDelete = () => {
    setTasks(prev => prev.filter(tk => tk.id !== taskId));
    closeModal();
    showToast('وظیفه حذف شد');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

function TaskAddContent() {

      id: 't' + Date.now(),

function NotifItem({
  notif: n,
  isRead,
  onMarkRead,
  onDelete
}) {
  const {
    closeModal,
    openChat,
    setAdminScreen,
    setCrmTab,
    setFinTab,
    agents
  } = useApp();
  const handleClick = () => {
    closeModal();
    if (n.type === 'chat') {
      const a = agents.find(x => x.id === n.target);
      if (a && !a.locked) setTimeout(() => openChat(n.target, 'agent'), 250);
    } else if (n.type === 'finance') {
      setAdminScreen('financeScreen');
      if (n.target === 'pending') setFinTab('invoices');
    } else if (n.type === 'crm') {
      setAdminScreen('crmScreen');
      if (n.target === 'leads') setCrmTab('leads');
    } else if (n.type === 'reports') {
      setAdminScreen('reportsScreen');
    }
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: `flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-[10px] mb-1.5 cursor-pointer border transition-all hover:border-[var(--aw-primary)] ${isRead ? 'border-transparent opacity-60' : 'border-[var(--aw-border)]'}`,
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    },
    onClick: handleClick
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "w-9 h-9 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-eu-nav-bg)',
      border: '1px solid var(--aw-eu-nav-border)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(8px)'
    }
  }, NOTIF_ICON_SRC[n.icon] ? /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("img", {
    src: NOTIF_ICON_SRC[n.icon],
    alt: "",
    className: "nav-icon-img",
    style: {
      width: 22,
      height: 22,
      objectFit: 'contain'
    }
  }) : /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: `${n.icon} text-sm`,
    style: {
      color: 'var(--aw-primary)'
    }
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-1.5"
  }, !isRead && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "w-2 h-2 rounded-full flex-shrink-0",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)'
    }
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[13px] truncate",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, n.title)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-secondary)]"
  }, n.desc), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[10px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] mt-1"
  }, n.time), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-2 mt-1.5"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-primary)] inline-block",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, n.cta, " \u2190"), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1"
  }), !isRead && onMarkRead && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] transition-all hover:opacity-80",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-hover)',
      color: 'var(--aw-success)',
      fontWeight: 600
    },
    onClick: e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      onMarkRead(n.id);
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-check text-[9px]"
  }), "\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647 \u0634\u062F"), onDelete && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] transition-all hover:opacity-80",
    style: {

    },
    onClick: e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      onDelete(n.id);
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-trash text-[9px]"
  }), "\u062D\u0630\u0641"))));
}
function NotificationsContent() {
  const [readIds, setReadIds] = useState(new Set());
  const [deletedIds, setDeletedIds] = useState(new Set());
  const handleMarkRead = id => setReadIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(id));
  const handleDelete = id => setDeletedIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(id));
  const handleMarkAllRead = () => setReadIds(new Set(NOTIFICATIONS.map(n => n.id)));
  const visibleNotifs = NOTIFICATIONS.filter(n => !deletedIds.has(n.id));
  const unreadCount = visibleNotifs.filter(n => !readIds.has(n.id)).length;
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, visibleNotifs.length > 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }, toFa(unreadCount), " \u0627\u0639\u0644\u0627\u0646 \u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0647\u200C\u0646\u0634\u062F\u0647"), unreadCount > 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "text-[11px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition-all hover:opacity-80",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-hover)',
      color: 'var(--aw-primary)',
      fontWeight: 600
    },
    onClick: handleMarkAllRead
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-check-double ml-1 text-[10px]"
  }), "\u062E\u0648\u0627\u0646\u062F\u0646 \u0647\u0645\u0647")), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(AnimatePresence, null, visibleNotifs.map(n => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(motion.div, {
    key: n.id,
    initial: {
      opacity: 1,
      height: 'auto'
    },
    exit: {
      opacity: 0,
      height: 0,
      marginBottom: 0
    },
    transition: {
      duration: 0.25
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(NotifItem, {
    notif: n,
    isRead: readIds.has(n.id),
    onMarkRead: handleMarkRead,
    onDelete: handleDelete
  })))), visibleNotifs.length === 0 && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-center py-10 text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {

  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[13px]"
  }, "\u0627\u0639\u0644\u0627\u0646\u06CC \u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F \u0646\u062F\u0627\u0631\u062F")));
}
function SearchContent() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const {
    agents,
    personnel,
    customers,
    deals,
    closeModal,
    openChat,
    openModal
  } = useApp();
  const results = [];
  if (query.trim()) {
    agents.filter(a => a.name.includes(query) || a.role.includes(query)).forEach(a => {
      results.push({
        name: a.name,
        sub: a.role,
        icon: 'fa-solid fa-robot',
        bg: a.bg,
        init: a.init,
        action: () => {
          closeModal();
          if (!a.locked) setTimeout(() => openChat(a.id, 'agent'), 200);
        }
      });
    });
    personnel.filter(p => p.name.includes(query) || p.role.includes(query)).forEach(p => {
      results.push({
        name: p.name,
        sub: p.role,
        icon: 'fa-solid fa-user-tie',
        bg: p.bg,
        init: p.init,
        action: () => {
          closeModal();
          setTimeout(() => openChat(p.id, 'personnel'), 200);
        }
      });
    });
    customers.filter(c => c.name.includes(query) || c.contact.includes(query)).forEach(c => {
      results.push({
        name: c.name,
        sub: c.contact,
        icon: 'fa-solid fa-user',
        bg: 'aw-bg-cyan',
        init: c.name[0],
        action: () => {
          closeModal();
          setTimeout(() => openModal(c.name, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(CustomerDetailContent, {
            customerId: c.id
          })), 200);
        }
      });
    });
    deals.filter(d => d.title.includes(query) || d.customer.includes(query)).forEach(d => {
      results.push({
        name: d.title,
        sub: d.customer,
        icon: 'fa-solid fa-handshake',
        bg: 'aw-bg-green',
        init: d.title[0],
        action: () => {
          closeModal();
          setTimeout(() => openModal(d.title, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(DealDetailContent, {
            dealId: d.id
          })), 200);
        }
      });
    });
  }
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] px-3 border border-[var(--aw-border)]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
    }
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-search text-sm text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("input", {
    className: "flex-1 bg-transparent border-none py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[var(--aw-text-primary)] outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]",
    placeholder: "\u062C\u0633\u062A\u062C\u0648 \u062F\u0631 \u0647\u0645\u0647 \u0628\u062E\u0634\u200C\u0647\u0627...",
    value: query,
    onChange: e => setQuery(e.target.value),
    autoFocus: true
  }), query && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "bg-transparent border-none text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] cursor-pointer text-sm",
    onClick: () => setQuery('')
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-times"
  }))), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "mt-3"
  }, results.length > 0 ? results.map((r, i) => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    key: i,
    className: "flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-[10px] mb-1 cursor-pointer border border-[var(--aw-border)] hover:border-[var(--aw-primary)]",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
    },
    onClick: r.action
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: `w-7 h-7 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[11px] ${r.bg}`,
    style: {
      fontWeight: 700
    }

function NewChatContent() {
  const {
    chatTab,
    setChatTab,
    agents,
    customers,
    setCustomers,
    personnel,
    setPersonnel,
    company,
    closeModal,
    showToast,
    openChat
  } = useApp();
  // Three list sections: کارکنان (personnel), مخاطبان (contacts), عامل‌ها (agents)
  const initList = chatTab === 'personnel' ? 'personnel' : chatTab === 'agents' ? 'agents' : 'customers';
  const [listTab, setListTab] = useState(initList);
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState('add');
  const [agentTab, setAgentTab] = useState('mine');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('');
  const listTabs = [{
    id: 'personnel',
    label: 'کارکنان',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-id-badge'
  }, {
    id: 'customers',
    label: 'مخاطبان',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-address-book'
  }, {
    id: 'agents',
    label: 'عامل‌ها',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-robot'
  }];

  // Agents split: mine vs other companies'
  const myAgents = agents.filter(a => (!a.company || a.company === company) && !a.locked);
  const otherAgents = agents.filter(a => a.company && a.company !== company);
  const sectionTabs = listTab === 'personnel' ? [{
    id: 'add',
    label: 'افزودن پرسنل جدید',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-user-plus'
  }, {
    id: 'select',
    label: 'انتخاب از لیست',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-list'
  }] : [{
    id: 'add',
    label: 'افزودن مخاطب جدید',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-user-plus'
  }, {
    id: 'select',
    label: 'انتخاب از لیست',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-list'
  }];
  const startAgentChat = id => {
    closeModal();
    setChatTab('agents');
    setTimeout(() => openChat(id, 'agent'), 100);
  };
  const saveAndChat = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    if (listTab === 'personnel') {
      const newId = 'p' + Date.now();
      const newPerson = {
        id: newId,
        name: name.trim(),
        role: role.trim() || 'کارمند',
        status: 'online',
        bg: 'aw-bg-indigo',
        init: name.trim()[0],
        lastMsg: '',
        lastTime: 'الان',
        unread: 0,
        voip: phone.trim()
      };
      setPersonnel(prev => [...prev, newPerson]);
      setChatTab('personnel');
      closeModal();
      showToast('پرسنل جدید اضافه شد');
      setTimeout(() => openChat(newId, 'personnel'), 100);
    } else {
      const newId = 'c' + Date.now();
      const newCustomer = {
        id: newId,
        name: name.trim(),
        contact: name.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        status: 'active',
        value: '۰',
        lastContact: 'امروز'
      };
      setCustomers(prev => [...prev, newCustomer]);
      setChatTab('customers');
      closeModal();
      showToast('مخاطب جدید اضافه شد');
      setTimeout(() => openChat(newId, 'customer'), 100);
    }
  };
  const existingList = listTab === 'personnel' ? personnel : customers;
  const agentRow = (a, badge) => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    key: a.id,
    className: "flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-[12px] cursor-pointer transition-all mb-1 border border-transparent hover:bg-[var(--aw-bg-card-hover)] hover:border-[var(--aw-primary)]",
    onClick: () => startAgentChat(a.id)
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(AgentCircle, {
    agent: a,
    size: 40,
    radius: 12,
    fontSize: 15
  }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex-1 min-w-0"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[13px] truncate",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 600
    }
  }, a.name), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[11px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] truncate"
  }, badge ? badge + ' · ' : '', a.role)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
    className: "fa-solid fa-comment-dots text-[var(--aw-primary)] text-sm"
  }));
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "flex gap-1 p-1 rounded-[12px] mb-3",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)'
    }
  }, listTabs.map(t => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    key: t.id,
    className: `flex-1 py-2 text-center border-none rounded-[9px] text-[12px] cursor-pointer transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${listTab === t.id ? 'text-white' : 'bg-transparent text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]'}`,
    style: listTab === t.id ? {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',

function CrmAddContent() {
  const {
    crmTab,
    customers,
    setCustomers,
    deals,
    setDeals,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  // Deal fields
  const [dealTitle, setDealTitle] = useState('');
  const [dealCustomer, setDealCustomer] = useState('');
  const [dealValue, setDealValue] = useState('');
  const [dealStage, setDealStage] = useState('negotiation');
  const saveCustomer = () => {
    if (!name) return;
    setCustomers(prev => [...prev, {
      id: 'c' + Date.now(),
      name,
      contact: name,
      phone,
      email,
      status: crmTab === 'leads' ? 'lead' : 'active',
      value: '۰',
      lastContact: 'امروز'
    }]);
    closeModal();
    showToast((crmTab === 'leads' ? 'سرنخ' : 'مشتری') + ' جدید اضافه شد');
  };
  const saveDeal = () => {
    if (!dealTitle) return;
    setDeals(prev => [...prev, {
      id: 'd' + Date.now(),
      title: dealTitle,
      customer: dealCustomer,
      value: dealValue,
      stage: dealStage,
      probability: dealStage === 'closed' ? '۱۰۰٪' : '۵۰٪'
    }]);
    closeModal();
    showToast('معامله جدید اضافه شد');
  };
  if (crmTab === 'deals') {
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {

      onChange: e => setDealCustomer(e.target.value),
      options: [{
        value: '',
        label: 'انتخاب مشتری...'
      }, ...customers.map(c => ({
        value: c.name,
        label: c.name
      }))]
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
      label: "\u0627\u0631\u0632\u0634 (\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0644)"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormInput, {
      placeholder: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A",
      value: dealValue,
      onChange: e => setDealValue(e.target.value)
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
      label: "\u0645\u0631\u062D\u0644\u0647"
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormSelect, {
      value: dealStage,
      onChange: e => setDealStage(e.target.value),
      options: [{
        value: 'negotiation',
        label: 'مذاکره'
      }, {
        value: 'proposal',

function FinDetailContent({
  itemId
}) {
  const {
    financeData,
    setFinanceData,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const f = [...financeData.income, ...financeData.expense].find(x => x.id === itemId);
  if (!f) return null;
  const markPaid = () => {
    setFinanceData(prev => ({
      income: prev.income.map(fi => fi.id === itemId ? {
        ...fi,
        status: 'paid'
      } : fi),
      expense: prev.expense.map(fi => fi.id === itemId ? {
        ...fi,
        status: 'paid'
      } : fi)
    }));
    showToast('وضعیت به پرداخت شده تغییر یافت');
    closeModal();
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-center mb-4"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[28px]",
    style: {
      fontWeight: 800,
      color: f.status === 'paid' ? 'var(--aw-secondary)' : 'var(--aw-accent)'
    }
  }, f.amount), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)]"
  }, "\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0644")), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
    label: "\u0634\u0631\u062D"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    className: "w-full p-2.5 rounded-[10px] text-[13px] border border-[var(--aw-border)]",

  }, f.category)), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
    className: "px-2 py-0.5 rounded-[20px] text-[10px]",

function FinAddContent({
  type
}) {
  const {
    setFinanceData,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('');
  const save = () => {
    if (!desc || !amount) return;
    const newItem = {
      id: 'f' + Date.now(),
      desc,
      amount,
      date: 'امروز',
      status: 'pending',
      category: category || (type === 'income' ? 'فروش' : 'عمومی')
    };
    setFinanceData(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [type]: [...prev[type], newItem]
    }));
    closeModal();
    showToast((type === 'income' ? 'درآمد' : 'هزینه') + ' جدید ثبت شد');
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
    label: "\u0634\u0631\u062D"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormInput, {
    placeholder: "\u0634\u0631\u062D \u062A\u0631\u0627\u06A9\u0646\u0634",
    value: desc,
    onChange: e => setDesc(e.target.value)
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
    label: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A (\u0631\u06CC\u0627\u0644)"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormInput, {
    placeholder: "\u0645\u0628\u0644\u063A",
    value: amount,
    onChange: e => setAmount(e.target.value)
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormGroup, {
    label: "\u062F\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC"
  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(FormInput, {
    placeholder: "\u062F\u0633\u062A\u0647\u200C\u0628\u0646\u062F\u06CC",
    value: category,
    onChange: e => setCategory(e.target.value)
  })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    className: "w-full py-2.5 px-5 border-none rounded-[10px] text-[13px] text-white cursor-pointer",
    style: {
      background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
      fontWeight: 600
    },
    onClick: save
  }, "\u062B\u0628\u062A"));
}

function CompaniesSettingsContent() {
  const {
    companies,

    openModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const reopenSelf = () => openModal('تنظیمات شرکت‌ها', /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(CompaniesSettingsContent, null));
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

    const active = k === company;
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      key: k,
      onClick: () => openModal('تنظیمات: ' + v.name, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(CompanyDetailSettings, {
        companyKey: k,
        onBack: reopenSelf
      }), reopenSelf),
      className: "w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-[10px] cursor-pointer border text-right transition-colors",
      style: {
        background: active ? 'var(--aw-primary-bg)' : 'var(--aw-bg-card)',

      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      className: "w-10 h-10 rounded-[10px] flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0",

function AddCompanyContent() {
  const {
    addCompany,
    closeModal,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [type, setType] = useState('تولیدی');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
  const inputStyle = {
    background: 'var(--aw-bg-input)',

    color: 'var(--aw-text-primary)',
    borderRadius: 12,

    fontSize: 13,
    width: '100%',
    outline: 'none',
    fontFamily: "'Kamand', 'Vazirmatn', sans-serif"
  };
  const submit = () => {

export function AgentSettingsContent({
  onBack
} = {}) {
  const {
    agents,
    company,
    openModal,
    closeModal,
    defaultAgent,
    setDefaultAgent,
    showToast
  } = useApp();
  const companyAgents = agents.filter(a => !a.company || a.company === company).sort((a, b) => (b.id === defaultAgent ? 1 : 0) - (a.id === defaultAgent ? 1 : 0));
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const reopenSelf = () => openModal('تنظیمات عامل‌ها', /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(AgentSettingsContent, {
    onBack: onBack
  }), onBack);
  const canSave = !!selected && selected !== defaultAgent;
  // The 6 fixed role categories — match by canonical team, then role keyword / id
  const ROLE_CATS = [{
    key: 'secretary',
    label: 'منشی',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-calendar-days',
    kw: 'منشی',
    ids: ['secretary', 'm1']
  }, {
    key: 'marketing',
    label: 'بازاریاب',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-bullhorn',
    kw: 'بازاریاب',
    ids: ['marketing', 'm2']
  }, {
    key: 'procurement',
    label: 'خرید/تدارکات',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-boxes-stacked',
    kw: 'تدارکات',
    ids: ['procurement', 'm6']
  }, {
    key: 'finance',
    label: 'مالی/اداری',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-sack-dollar',
    kw: 'مالی',
    ids: ['finance', 'm3']
  }, {
    key: 'sales',
    label: 'فروشنده/صندوقدار',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-cart-shopping',
    kw: 'فروش',
    ids: ['sales', 'm7']
  }, {
    key: 'assistant',
    label: 'دستیار شخصی',
    icon: 'fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles',
    kw: 'دستیار',
    ids: ['assistant', 'm0']
  }];
  const catKey = a => {
    const team = a.team;
    if (team && ROLE_CATS.some(c => c.key === team)) return team;
    const c = ROLE_CATS.find(c => (a.role || '').includes(c.kw) || c.ids.some(id => (a.id || '').includes(id)));
    return c ? c.key : null;
  };
  const roleGroups = {};
  companyAgents.forEach(a => {
    const k = catKey(a);
    if (k) (roleGroups[k] = roleGroups[k] || []).push(a);
  });
  const renderAgentRow = a => {
    const checked = selected ? selected === a.id : defaultAgent === a.id;
    return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
      key: a.id,
      className: "flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5 rounded-[10px] mb-1 cursor-pointer border border-[var(--aw-border)] hover:border-[var(--aw-primary)]",
      style: {
        background: 'var(--aw-bg-card)'
      },
      onClick: () => {
        closeModal();
        setTimeout(() => openModal('تنظیمات: ' + a.name, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(CustomizeAgentContent, {
          agentId: a.id
        }), reopenSelf), 200);
      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
      onClick: e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        setSelected(prev => prev === a.id ? null : a.id);
      },
      title: "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u062E\u0627\u0628 \u0628\u0647\u200C\u0639\u0646\u0648\u0627\u0646 \u0639\u0627\u0645\u0644 \u067E\u06CC\u0634\u200C\u0641\u0631\u0636",
      className: "w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer flex-none border-none p-0",
      style: {
        boxShadow: checked ? 'inset 0 0 0 2px var(--aw-primary)' : 'inset 0 0 0 2px var(--aw-border)',
        background: checked ? 'var(--aw-primary)' : 'transparent'
      }
    }, checked && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-check text-white text-[10px]"
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement(AgentCircle, {
      agent: a,
      size: 32,
      radius: 8,
      fontSize: 13
    }), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      className: "flex-1 text-[13px]",
      style: {
        fontWeight: 500
      }
    }, a.name), defaultAgent === a.id && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      className: "text-[9px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-white flex-none",
      style: {
        background: 'var(--aw-primary, #2E86FF)',
        fontWeight: 700
      }
    }, "\u067E\u06CC\u0634\u200C\u0641\u0631\u0636"), a.locked && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {
      className: "text-[10px] text-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md",

      }
    }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-lock"
    })), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("i", {
      className: "fa-solid fa-chevron-left text-[12px] text-[var(--aw-text-muted)] flex-none"
    }));
  };
  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, ROLE_CATS.filter(c => roleGroups[c.key]?.length).map(c => /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {
    key: c.key,

  }, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", {

    style: {

  }, c.label), /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("span", {

    }
  })), roleGroups[c.key].map(renderAgentRow))), canSave && /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
    onClick: () => {
      setDefaultAgent(selected);
      const ag = companyAgents.find(x => x.id === selected);
      showToast('عامل پیش‌فرض ذخیره شد' + (ag ? ': ' + ag.name : ''));

function SingleAgentSettings({

  return /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("div", null, /*#__PURE__*/React.createElement("button", {
